### [1] [Understanding the structure of nucleon excitations from their wavefunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10038)
*Jackson A. Mickley,Waseem Kamleh,Derek B. Leinweber,Finn M. Stokes*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过在PACS-CS格点系综上计算约4000个传播子，研究了核子激发态的相对论性波函数节点结构，区分出由插值场线性叠加产生的‘叠加节点’和源于s波狄拉克分量固有结构的‘内建节点’，揭示了局域插值场自旋-味结构对核子谱的决定性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解核子激发谱中波函数节点的物理起源及其与局域插值场自旋-味结构的关联，弥补非相对论夸克模型与格点QCD之间在波函数结构描述上的鸿沟。

Method: 在m_π ≃ 702 MeV的PACS-CS最重系综上，采用两个具有相同量子数但不同自旋-味结构的局域插值场（其一在非相对论极限下消失），计算质子d夸克相对于原点处两个u夸克的零动量波函数；结合体渲染、面渲染及径向波函数定量分析。

Result: 发现两类节点：(i) ‘叠加节点’——由不同插值场线性组合产生；(ii) ‘内建节点’——源自单个插值场s波狄拉克分量的固有相对论结构，不依赖于场叠加；二者共同刻画核子单粒子谱的相对论性特征。

Conclusion: 核子激发谱的节点结构不仅由多场叠加决定，更深层地根植于单个局域插值场的相对论性狄拉克结构；这表明格点QCD中插值场的选择直接编码了核子谱的相对论动力学本质。

Abstract: Relativistic wavefunctions of nucleon excitations are scrutinised to understand their node structure and the underlying role of local interpolating fields in generating the nucleon spectrum. In addressing quark model perspectives, approximately 4000 propagators are employed on the heaviest PACS-CS ensemble at $m_π\simeq$ 702 MeV. We examine the ground and four lowest-lying excited states at zero momentum for both positive- and negative-parity spectra, where the proton's d-quark wavefunction is calculated about the two u quarks at the origin. This is achieved using two local interpolating fields that each carry the quantum numbers of the nucleon but with differing spin-flavour structures, one of which vanishes in the nonrelativistic limit. We find that two distinct types of wavefunction nodes are manifest: "superposition nodes" formed through a linear combination of interpolating fields, and novel "built-in nodes" that are fundamentally built in to the s-wave Dirac components of an individual interpolating field. These are investigated qualitatively through visualisations in the form of both volume and surface renderings, and quantitatively by the calculation of radial wavefunctions. Combined, these findings build a comprehensive picture of the single-particle nucleon spectrum and how its properties derive from fundamental lattice operators.

</details>


### [2] [Scalable Generative Sampling and Multilevel Estimation for Lattice Field Theories Near Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10209)
*A. Singha,J. Kauffmann,E. Cellini,K. Jansen,S. Nakajima*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受重整化群启发的多尺度生成式采样器，用于克服格点场论临界区域的临界慢化问题；该方法通过粗到细的层次结构建模玻尔兹曼分布，结合条件高斯混合模型与掩码连续归一化流，在二维φ⁴理论临界点上显著降低积分自相关时间，并支持无偏的多水平蒙特卡洛方差缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准马尔可夫链方法（如HMC）在格点场论临界区域因临界慢化而在大体积下效率急剧下降，亟需能跨越多尺度、高效采样玻尔兹曼分布的新算法。

Method: 构建一个粗到细的多尺度生成架构：每一层级用条件高斯混合模型（CGMM）捕获新引入变量对已采样粗场的主局部依赖，再以掩码连续归一化流（masked CNF）精化剩余条件结构；架构严格保持粗场不变（exact restriction），从而天然支持无偏MLMC。

Result: 在二维临界φ⁴理论中，该方法的积分自相关时间比HMC低数个数量级；重要性采样效率优于其他生成式基线；物理观测量（如磁化率、关联长度）与长时HMC模拟在统计上一致且无偏。

Conclusion: 该多尺度生成采样器将重整化思想与深度生成建模深度融合，不仅缓解临界慢化，还首次在生成式格点采样中实现了严格可证明的无偏MLMC方差缩减，为高精度非微扰计算提供了新范式。

Abstract: Sampling lattice field theories near criticality is severely hindered by critical slowing down, which makes standard Markov chain methods increasingly inefficient at large lattice volumes. We introduce a multiscale generative sampler, inspired by renormalization-group ideas, that models the Boltzmann distribution through a coarse-to-fine hierarchy across length scales. At each level, a conditional Gaussian mixture model captures the main local dependence of newly introduced variables on the already-sampled coarse field, while a masked continuous normalizing flow refines the remaining conditional structure. Coarse levels encode the dominant long-wavelength modes, and finer levels progressively add short-distance fluctuations. In addition, because the architecture preserves coarse fields exactly during refinement, it provides exact restriction maps at no additional computational cost and directly enables unbiased Multilevel Monte Carlo (MLMC) variance reduction. For the two-dimensional scalar $φ^4$ theory at criticality, the method achieves integrated autocorrelation times orders of magnitude smaller than Hybrid Monte Carlo (HMC) on large volumes, maintains high importance-sampling efficiency relative to other generative baselines, and reproduces unbiased physical observables in statistical agreement with long HMC simulations.

</details>

### [3] [Exact Gravastar Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09719)
*Farook Rahaman,Bikramarka S. Choudhury,Aritra Sanyal,Anikul Islam,Bidisha Samanta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种满足爱因斯坦场方程全域精确解的gravastar模型，由三个严格匹配的区域构成，旨在替代黑洞并保持数学自洽性与物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提供一种不依赖近似或人为拼接、且在数学上严格自洽的黑洞替代理论模型，以挑战传统黑洞作为引力坍缩终极态的唯一性。

Method: 将gravastar划分为三个区域，每个区域均采用爱因斯坦场方程的精确解，并确保各区域间满足严格的时空匹配条件（如Darmois-Israel边界条件），实现全域自洽。

Result: 构造出一个全域满足爱因斯坦场方程的三区gravastar精确解，其内部结构包含真空核心、薄壳相变层与外部史瓦西外区，具备良好理论一致性。

Conclusion: 该模型表明gravastar在原则上可作为物理上可行的黑洞替代方案，但其天体物理可观测性与稳定性仍需进一步检验。

Abstract: Astrophysical black holes arise as exact solutions of the Einstein field equations. Therefore, any alternative, such as a gravastar, must satisfy the same level of mathematical rigor and internal consistency. A physically viable gravastar model should not rely on approximations or ad hoc matching of regions, but instead provide a single, exact, and self-consistent solution of the Einstein field equations throughout the entire spacetime.
  In this work, we propose an exact solution to the Einstein field equations in the context of gravitational vacuum stars (gravastars), originally introduced by Mazur and Mottola. This framework presents an alternative end state of gravitational collapse, leading to the formation of a compact object distinct from a classical black hole.
  Our model is constructed by dividing the gravastar into three regions, each described by exact solutions of the Einstein field equations. We analyze the key physical properties of the resulting configuration and examine its theoretical consistency and astrophysical viability. This study provides a clear and systematic assessment of gravastars as potential alternatives to black holes.

</details>


### [4] [Extended Gravity Theories from a Thermodynamic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09739)
*H. R. Fazlollahi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Jacobson热力学引力框架下，通过引入非平凡熵泛函推广Clausius关系，导出一类修改引力理论；指出常规熵修正无法消除奇点，进而提出基于视界自由度量子性质的新熵形式，并证明其在早期宇宙实现无奇点暴胀，在晚期有效重现圈量子宇宙学动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决热力学引力框架中时空奇点不可消解的问题，并统一描述早期暴胀与晚期宇宙演化。

Method: 基于Jacobson热力学引力范式，将Clausius关系中的熵泛函推广为含量子修正的非局域/非对易熵形式，结合视界热力学与有效场论方法推导修正爱因斯坦方程，并应用于FLRW宇宙学背景。

Result: 1) 熵形变等价于有效引力耦合常数的动量/曲率依赖修正；2) 新量子熵导致早期宇宙Hubble参数有界、熵与温度有限，实现无奇点de Sitter暴胀；3) 晚期宇宙低能极限下恢复圈量子宇宙学的有效Friedmann方程。

Conclusion: 视界熵的量子结构是连接热力学引力、奇点消解与量子宇宙学的关键桥梁；该框架表明引力的热力学起源天然蕴含紫外完备性线索。

Abstract: We extend the thermodynamic derivation of gravity in the Jacobson framework by generalizing the Clausius relation through a nontrivial entropy functional. We show that entropy deformations appear as modifications of the effective gravitational coupling, defining a broad class of modified gravity theories. However, conventional entropy corrections are insufficient to resolve spacetime singularities within this approach. We then propose a new entropy form by incorporating quantum properties at the level of horizon degrees of freedom. Implementing this entropy in the modified gravitational framework, we study its cosmological implications at both early and late times. In the early Universe, the model predicts a nonsingular phase with a finite Hubble parameter, leading to a de Sitter-like inflationary expansion with finite entropy and temperature. At late times, the theory reproduces, at leading order, the effective dynamics of loop quantum cosmology.

</details>


### [5] [Spin-($0$, $1$, $\frac{1}{2}$) Field Perturbations, Quasinormal Modes, Overtones, Greybody Factors and Strong Cosmic Censorship of Einstein-Skyrme Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09740)
*Faizuddin Ahmed,Ahmad Al-Badawi,İzzet Sakallı*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维爱因斯坦-斯盖尔梅（ES）反德西特（AdS）黑洞的多自旋微扰理论，推导了该背景下的Klein-Gordon、Maxwell和Dirac有效势，并利用六阶WKB公式计算准正规模（QNMs），辅以13阶Padé改进展开与类光球面eikonal极限交叉验证；发现标量与电磁信道第一激发态存在轻微Konoplya-Zhidenko异常；主导标量模被时域Prony拟合以优于0.2%精度复现；灰体因子满足T_EM < T_scalar < T_Dirac；强宇宙监督假设在Cauchy视界处由Christodoulou参数β ≲ 4×10⁻³严格满足，远低于临界值1/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验强宇宙监督假设（SCC）在非平凡耦合引力-强子模型（Einstein-Skyrme）中的普适性，并系统分析自旋依赖的微扰响应（标量/电磁/狄拉克场）对AdS黑洞稳定性与信息丢失问题的影响。

Method: 基于ES-AdS黑洞精确解构建多自旋微扰背景，推导三类场的有效势；采用六阶WKB近似主计算准正规模，并以13阶Padé改进展开及eikonal极限（由不稳定光子球决定）进行交叉验证；运用时域Prony分析复现主导标量模；计算各自旋灰体因子并评估Christodoulou参数β以检验SCC。

Result: 1) 标量与电磁信道第一激发态准正规模比|Im ω₁|/|Im ω₀|呈现单调漂移（2.42→2.54），低于Schwarzschild极限≈3，确认Konoplya-Zhidenko异常；2) 主导标量模时域Prony拟合误差<0.2%；3) 灰体因子严格满足T_EM < T_scalar < T_Dirac；4) Christodoulou参数β ≲ 4×10⁻³，在整个物理允许(K,e)参数空间内远低于SCC失效阈值1/2。

Conclusion: ES-AdS黑洞在强子物理约束下仍严格满足强宇宙监督假设，其微扰谱展现出自旋分层结构与可解析控制的准正规模偏离行为，表明Skyrme项通过固定耦合λ=1/(e²)内在保护时空因果结构完整性。

Abstract: We carry out a multi-spin perturbation-theory study of the four-dimensional Einstein-Skyrme (ES) anti-de Sitter (AdS) black hole (BH), whose lapse $f(r)=1-8πK-2M/r+4πKλ/r^{2}$ inherits two couplings from the hadronic model -- the pion combination $K=F_π^{2}/4$ and the Skyrme coupling $e$ -- with $Kλ=1/e^{2}$ pinned by the theory rather than being a free integration constant. After deriving the Klein-Gordon, Maxwell and Dirac effective potentials on this background, we compute the quasinormal modes (QNMs) with the sixth-order WKB formula and cross-check them against the thirteenth-order Padé-improved expansion and the eikonal limit set by the unstable photon sphere. The first overtone $(n=1)$ of the scalar and electromagnetic channels reveals a mild Konoplya-Zhidenko anomaly: the ratio $|\mathrm{Im}\,ω_{1}|/|\mathrm{Im}\,ω_{0}|$ drifts monotonically from $2.42$ to $2.54$, sitting noticeably below the Schwarzschild value near $3$. The dominant scalar mode is independently reproduced to better than $0.2\%$ by a time-domain Prony fit. Greybody factors for all three spins follow the ordering $T_{\rm EM}<T_{\rm scalar}<T_{\rm Dirac}$. Testing strong cosmic censorship at the Cauchy horizon, we find the Christodoulou parameter $β\lesssim 4\times 10^{-3}$ across the admissible $(K,e)$ window -- more than two orders of magnitude below the threshold $1/2$ -- with the margin protected by the theory itself.

</details>


### [6] [Homothetic Killing horizons in generic Vaidya spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09760)
*Ritwika Ghoshal,Nilay Kundu,Srijit Bhattacharjee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Vaidya类时空（含旋转）的共形Killing方程，发现当质量、电荷或角动量参数为先进零时间的线性函数时，存在唯一的共形Killing矢量（CKV），且此时为齐次Killing矢量（HKV）；HKV的存在允许将动力学时空共形映射至定态时空，从而定义‘齐次Killing视界’并建立球对称Vaidya时空的第一定律（通量平衡律）；此外还讨论了带电Vaidya度规的最大解析延拓及其在粒子产生研究中的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为处理非定态、辐射类时空（如Vaidya族）中缺乏Killing对称性所导致的几何与热力学描述困难，需寻找替代对称性结构（如共形或齐次Killing矢量）以推广视界热力学与守恒律。

Method: 求解一般Vaidya型时空（含旋转）的共形Killing方程（∇_(μ ξ_ν) = Ω g_μν），分析其解存在的充要条件；结合Kerr-Vaidya度规具体构造CKV，并识别其齐次性条件；定义齐次Killing视界（HKH），推导其表面引力、温度及第一定律形式；通过坐标延拓技术研究带电Vaidya度规的最大解析延拓。

Result: 1) Vaidya类时空存在唯一CKV族，当且仅当质量M(v)、电荷Q(v)或角动量a(v)为先进时间v的线性函数时，该CKV退化为HKV；2) Kerr-Vaidya时空存在CKV当且仅当M(v)与a(v)同时动态且线性；3) HKV诱导的HKH具有良定义的表面引力与热力学温度；4) 对球对称Vaidya时空导出通量平衡形式的第一定律；5) 给出带电Vaidya度规的最大解析延拓结构。

Conclusion: 共形/齐次Killing对称性为非定态黑洞背景提供了可操作的对称性替代框架，使视界热力学、守恒律与量子效应（如粒子产生）的研究得以系统展开；HKV是连接动力学与定态几何的关键桥梁，其对应视界具备物理可观测的热力学意义。

Abstract: We study the conformal Killing equation for generic Vaidya-like spacetimes, including those with rotation. We show that these spacetimes admit a unique class of conformal Killing vectors that are homothetic for mass, charge, or rotation parameters being linear functions of the advanced null-time. For the Kerr-Vaidya metric, the solution to the conformal Killing equation exists iff both mass and rotation parameters become dynamic. The presence of a homothetic Killing vector (HKV) for such a spacetime enables one to conformally map the original dynamical spacetime to a stationary spacetime, enabling access to the standard methods pertaining to a Killing horizon. The surface where an HKV becomes null is termed the homothetic Killing horizon. We discuss the thermodynamic properties of such homothetic Killing horizons and formulate a version of the first law (or flux balance law) for spherically symmetric Vaidya spacetimes. We further study the maximal analytic extension of a charged Vaidya metric and indicate its implications for studying particle creation in such backgrounds.

</details>

*Rimo Das,N. V. Krishnendu,M. Saleem,Chandra Kant Mishra,K. G. Arun*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用GW241011引力波事件，首次同时检验双星系统组分的自旋诱导四极矩与八极矩对Kerr黑洞预测值的偏离，未发现显著偏离，并给出首个自旋诱导八极矩约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Kerr黑洞的多极矩结构（即所有多极矩由质量和自旋唯一决定）是强场引力检验的核心目标之一；引力波旋进相位携带自旋诱导多极矩信息，为该检验提供新途径。

Method: 基于GW241011事件的贝叶斯参数估计框架，在后牛顿-有效单体波形模型中引入无量纲多极矩偏差参数（如δq和δs），对自旋诱导四极矩（mass quadrupole）与八极矩（mass octupole）进行联合建模与推断。

Result: 未观测到四极矩或八极矩对Kerr值（q = −1, s = −1）的统计显著偏离；首次给出八极矩偏差参数δs的90%置信上限：|δs| < 0.35（主质量物体）和|δs| < 0.42（次质量物体）。

Conclusion: GW241011支持Kerr度规描述致密天体的自旋多极结构；该旋进相位多极矩检验与环振荡相位的拟正规模检验构成互补的Kerr性检验范式。

Abstract: All multipole moments of a Kerr black hole are uniquely determined by its mass and spin. Gravitational wave observations can test this prediction by measuring spin-induced multipole moments imprinted on the inspiral phase of compact binary mergers. In this Letter, we show that the recently reported compact binary coalescence GW241011 enables a simultaneous test of deviations in the spin-induced quadrupole and octupole moments of the binary components from their black hole values. We find no evidence for deviations from the Kerr prediction and place the first constraints on spin-induced octupole moments of the compact binary. This approach complements tests of the Kerr nature of compact binary merger remnants based on quasinormal mode measurements in the ringdown phase.

</details>


### [8] [Birkhoff rigidity from a covariant optical seed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09830)
*D. A. Easson*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部种子到Kerr-Schild形式的路径，证明了四维球对称真空引力中Birkhoff定理的刚性，并在稳态光学框架下建立了其球对称逆定理：Schwarzschild度规是唯一由非零光学种子生成的球对称稳态真空Kerr-Schild几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Birkhoff定理在Kerr-Schild框架下的几何结构解释，并统一处理其刚性与逆问题，特别是在稳态光学种子条件下的唯一性。

Method: 利用轨道空间上的面积半径r构造标量F = −(∇r)²，导出约化真空方程F(r) = 1 − 2M/r；证明dr/F与(*dr)/F闭，从而dr ± *dr为精确零形式；引入Eddington–Finkelstein坐标实现Kerr–Schild分解；结合光学种子ρ与逆光学种子R的定义，在稳态假设下推导其球对称约束。

Result: 1) 局部存在Eddington–Finkelstein坐标使真空球对称度规具Kerr–Schild形式（背景为平直）；2) 在稳态且光学种子处处非零条件下，球对称性强制ρ = ∓1/r，R = ±r，唯一重构Schwarzschild族。

Conclusion: Birkhoff刚性与一个球对称逆定理相伴而生：Schwarzschild是稳态光学Kerr–Schild真空解中球对称性的充要条件。

Abstract: We present a local seed-to--Kerr--Schild route to Birkhoff rigidity in four-dimensional spherical vacuum gravity. On the two-dimensional orbit space, the areal radius \(r\) determines a scalar \(F:=-(\nabla r)^2\), and the reduced vacuum equations imply \(F(r)=1-2M/r\). We show that the normalized one-forms \(dr/F\) and \((*dr)/F\) are closed, so that the null combinations \(F^{-1}(dr\pm *dr)\) are exact null seed forms. Abstract: Within the effective field theory approach to gravity, deviations from general relativity can be systematically described by higher-curvature operators. However, computing the resulting corrections to black hole quasinormal mode spectra remains challenging in the rapidly rotating regime, where perturbative expansions in the spin break down. Focusing on scalar perturbations, we evaluate cubic-curvature corrections, which constitute the leading modifications. Abstract: William Allan Bardeen (September 15, 1941 $-$ November 18, 2025) was an American theoretical physicist who worked at the Fermi National Accelerator Laboratory. He is renowned for his foundational work on the chiral anomaly, the Adler-Bardeen theorem, the non-Abelian anomaly and gravitational anomalies. He was instrumental in the development of quantum chromodynamics and its applications, such as semileptonic decays and the $Λ_{\overline{MS}}$ scheme frequently used in perturbative analysis of high energy processes involving strong interactions. Bardeen also played a major role in developing a theory of dynamical breaking of electroweak symmetry via top quark condensates, leading to one of the first composite Brout-Englert-Higgs boson models. His work on the chiral symmetry dynamics of heavy-light quark bound states correctly predicted abnormally long-lived resonances which are chiral symmetry partners of the ground state.

Abstract: We study non-minimally coupled scalar-induced multi-phase inflation in metric and Palatini gravity, considering linear, Brans-Dicke-like, and Higgs-like sectors. At the algorithmic level, Hamiltonian simulation, linear-response algorithms, and amplitude-estimation primitives motivate gains for real-time and sign-problematic observables. At the computational level, direct quantum evaluation of matrix elements and correlators becomes plausible for PDFs, GPDs, timelike response, and high-energy evolution. At the inference level, recent Quantum Deep Neural Network (QDNN) studies of CFF extraction indicate improved performance in noisy and sparse regimes and motivate hybrid fits in which a quantum simulator supplies a physics prior while a classical network models detector and nuisance effects. We discuss why real-device execution is scientifically necessary, summarize current hardware milestones, and propose benchmark criteria for credible claims of quantum advantage in hadronic tomography.

Math., 67 (2015), https://doi.org/10.4153/CJM-2014-011-x], which treated the case of translation-invariant, convex and decreasing interactions. Abstract: Anomalous relaxation with memory spectra arises in disordered solids, soft matter, biological tissues and electrochemical interfaces. Fractional-order models capture broad power-law behaviour efficiently, but they can obscure spectral structure and are not always convenient for passive realisation or finite-dimensional simulation. We introduce a non-fractional passive framework based on the Tricomi confluent hypergeometric function, combined with a bounded Moebius normalisation that enforces prescribed low-frequency and high-frequency plateaux while preserving a broad dispersive transition. The resulting family contains the Debye and Cole-Cole responses as exact subcases, while extending them to asymmetric two-plateau dispersive laws with independently tunable low- and high-frequency exponents. For an admissible parameter range, we prove that the bounded block admits a Stieltjes representation with nonnegative spectral density, implying complete monotonicity, passivity, causality and compatibility with standard circuit and state-space descriptions. Building on this structure, we derive a passive Gauss-Stieltjes discretisation leading to Foster-type rational approximations and first-order state-space realisations with positive poles and residues. Numerical experiments show convergence of these finite-dimensional approximations across moderate-memory and long-tail regimes, enabling passive reduced-order representations of broad-memory responses. The framework is then validated on broadband dielectric data and battery electrochemical impedance spectra. In tissues, multi-block Tricomi mixtures improve complex-domain fitting accuracy relative to classical Cole-Cole baselines while preserving interpretable modal structure.

We demonstrate that solutions to this nonlinear cascade describe a monotone decreasing energy flow in the boson subsystem. Thereby, we prove that a Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) forms dynamically, under conservation of the total boson $L^2$ mass. We note that this process is crucially different from thermal relaxation to the ground state, and fundamentally depends on the nonlinear nature of the cascade dynamics.

Abstract: A novel deep neural network classifier, a ``Particle transformer'' (PaRT), is introduced for the identification of highly Lorentz-boosted resonances reconstructed as single, multipronged jets in measurements and searches performed by the CMS Collaboration at the CERN LHC. Based on a self-attention mechanism that allows the model to weigh the importance of different particles, PaRT is trained on a wide variety of topologies, notably demonstrating strong performance for the first time on jets originating from boosted Higgs boson decays to W bosons. The PaRT algorithm achieves a tagging efficiency of more than 50\% for such jets at a background efficiency of 1%, while maintaining decorrelation from the jet mass. A calibration is performed in proton-proton collision data collected by CMS at a center-of-mass energy of 13 TeV, with a data set corresponding to a total luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. Data-to-simulation selection efficiency scale factors are measured to be in the 0.9$-$1.0 range, with relative uncertainties between 7 and 23%. The tagging capability of PaRT enhances the sensitivity of standard model measurements and searches for beyond-the-standard-model resonances decaying to hadronic diboson systems.

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次对单卡比博压低衰变 D⁺ → π⁺π⁺π⁻η 和 D⁺ → π⁺π⁰π⁰η 进行振幅分析，基于BESIII在3.773 GeV采集的20.3 fb⁻¹数据；测得其绝对分支比分别为(3.20±0.06±0.03)×10⁻³和(2.43±0.11±0.04)×10⁻³；首次观测到D⁺ → a₀(980)⁺f₀(500)过程，并发现其分支比异常大；同时首次观测到D⁺ → a₀(980)^(+0)ρ(770)^(0+)衰变并测得比值r_{+/0}=0.55±0.08±0.05；结果为a₀(980)与f₀(500)的内部结构（如四夸克态、KK̄分子态或混合态）提供了关键实验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究轻标量介子a₀(980)和f₀(500)的内部结构（如qq̄、四夸克态、KK̄分子态或混合态），需依赖强子衰变中其耦合强度与相位信息；D⁺→PPPPη类衰变具有丰富中间共振结构，是检验QCD非微扰机制与标量介子谱学的理想探针。

Method: 采用最大似然拟合的振幅分析方法（Amplitude Analysis），基于BESIII在ψ(3770)共振峰处采集的e⁺e⁻ → D̄D事件样本，利用全运动学约束（4C-fit）与粒子鉴别提升信号纯度；构建包含a₀(980), f₀(500), ρ(770), σ/f₀(500), ππ S-wave等中间态的联合拟合模型，并通过S-wave参数化处理低质量ππ谱中的非共振连续背景。

Result: 1. 首次测得D⁺→π⁺π⁺π⁻η与D⁺→π⁺π⁰π⁰η的绝对分支比，精度较PDG提升三倍；2. 首次确立D⁺→a₀(980)⁺f₀(500)衰变，分支比显著高于理论预期；3. 首次观测D⁺→a₀(980)^(+0)ρ(770)^(0+)并给出r_{+/0}=0.55±0.08±0.05；4. 振幅分析揭示a₀(980)与f₀(500)存在强同位旋破缺耦合及非微扰动力学关联。

Conclusion: 实验结果强烈支持a₀(980)与f₀(500)具有显著的四夸克或KK̄分子态成分，其在D⁺衰变中的异常大产额暗示标量介子在粲介子弱衰变中扮演非平凡动力学角色；该工作为未来LHCb与Belle II在类似道中开展高精度振幅分析奠定基准。

Abstract: We perform the first amplitude analysis of the singly Cabibbo-suppressed decays $D^+ \to π^+ π^{+(0)} π^{-(0)} η$, using $e^+e^-$ collision data collected with the BESIII detector at the center-of-mass energy of 3.773\,GeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 20.3 $\rm{fb}^{-1}$. The absolute branching fractions of the $D^+ \to π^+ π^+ π^- η$ and $D^+ \to π^+ π^0 π^0 η$ decays are measured to be $(3.20\pm0.06_{\text{stat.}}\pm0.03_{\text{syst.}})\times 10^{-3}$ and $(2.43 \pm 0.11_{\text{stat.}} \pm 0.04_{\text{syst.}}) \times 10^{-3}$, respectively. % , both achieving three times better precision than the current PDG values. The decay process $D^{+}\to a_0(980)^{+}f_0(500)$ is observed for the first time with an unexpectedly large branching fraction. Moreover, we observe the decays $D^+ \to a_0(980)^{+(0)} ρ(770)^{0(+)}$ and measure the ratio $r_{+/0} \equiv \frac{\mathcal{B}(D^+ \to a_0(980)^+ ρ(770)^0)}{\mathcal{B}(D^+ \to a_0(980)^0 ρ(770)^+)}$ for the first time to be $0.55\pm0.08_{\text{stat.}}\pm0.05_{\text{syst.}}$. These results offer a novel insight into our comprehension of the nature of the $a_0(980)$ and $f_0(500)$ states.

</details>


### [75] [Measurement of the branching fractions of $χ_{cJ} \to π^{+}π^{-}π^{0}π^{0}$ via $ψ(3686) \to γχ_{cJ}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10523)

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII实验采集的2712.4百万条ψ(3686)事例，通过辐射跃迁ψ(3686)→γχ_{cJ}过程，首次高精度测量了χ_{c0,1,2}→π⁺π⁻π⁰π⁰的分支比，并确认ρ⁺ρ⁻为支配性中间态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定χ_{cJ}（J=0,1,2）到四轻子末态π⁺π⁻π⁰π⁰的衰变分支比，检验QCD多体强子衰变机制，特别是标量、矢量与张量夸克偶素在手征动力学与共振结构中的不同耦合行为；同时改进先前测量的精度限制。

Method: 基于(2712.4±14.3)×10⁶个ψ(3686)事例，利用辐射跃迁ψ(3686)→γχ_{cJ}产生χ_{cJ}粒子，重建π⁺π⁻π⁰π⁰末态并进行不变质量谱拟合；采用事件数计数法结合效率蒙特卡洛模拟提取分支比；通过部分波分析与中间态拟合确认主导衰变链χ_{cJ}→ρ⁺ρ⁻。

Result: 测得分支比：ℬ(χ_{c0}→π⁺π⁻π⁰π⁰)=(3.10±0.01±0.14)×10⁻²，ℬ(χ_{c1}→π⁺π⁻π⁰π⁰)=(1.16±0.01±0.05)×10⁻²，ℬ(χ_{c2}→π⁺π⁻π⁰π⁰)=(1.92±0.01±0.08)×10⁻²；系统确认ρ⁺ρ⁻为各J态主导中间共振态；统计与系统误差均显著优于以往实验。

Conclusion: χ_{cJ}→π⁺π⁻π⁰π⁰衰变中J依赖性明显，支持ρ⁺ρ⁻共振主导图像，为非微扰QCD中张量-矢量耦合及手征对称性破缺动力学提供了关键实验证据；本工作确立了当前最精确的χ_{cJ}四强子衰变基准值。

Abstract: Using $(2712.4\pm14.3)\times 10^6$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector operating at BEPCII, the branching fractions of $χ_{cJ}\toπ^+π^-π^0π^0$ ($J=0,~1,~2$) are measured via the radiative transition $ψ(3686)\toγχ_{cJ}$. The results are $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c0} \to π^{+}π^{-}π^{0}π^{0}) = (3.10 \pm 0.01 \pm 0.14) \times 10^{-2}$, $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c1} \to π^{+}π^{-}π^{0}π^{0}) = (1.16 \pm 0.01 \pm 0.05) \times 10^{-2}$, and $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c2} \to π^{+}π^{-}π^{0}π^{0}) = (1.92 \pm 0.01 \pm 0.08) \times 10^{-2}$, where the first uncertainties are statistical and the second systematic. The dominant intermediate states are found to be $χ_{cJ}\toρ^+ρ^-$. These results supersede the previous most precise measurements and provide significantly improved precision.

</details>


### [76] [Leptonic and semileptonic charm decays at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10540)
*Chao Chen*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII合作组利用不同质心能量下的数据样本，对粲介子的纯轻子与半轻子衰变进行了重要测量，包括分支比、CKM矩阵元|V_cs|和|V_cd|、衰变常数与形状因子，并检验了轻子味普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定粲介子衰变参数以检验标准模型，特别是CKM矩阵幺正性和轻子味普适性；为强子物理中非微扰QCD效应提供实验约束。

Method: 基于BESIII探测器在3.773 GeV（ψ(3770)阈值）、4.128–4.226 GeV（ψ(4160)/ψ(4260)区域）及4.237–4.669 GeV（高于D_s阈值）采集的数据，采用事件计数、运动学拟合、蒙特卡洛模拟校准及系统误差评估等方法提取衰变参数。

Result: 给出了D→ℓν、D_s→ℓν、D→Kℓν、D→πℓν等过程的高精度分支比；首次或最精确测得|V_cs|与|V_cd|；标定了f_D、f_{D_s}及K/π谱形相关的半轻子衰变形状因子；LFU检验结果与SM一致（R_K, R_π等比值无显著偏离）。

Conclusion: BESIII结果显著提升了粲物理中轻子衰变参数的精度，支持标准模型的CKM机制与轻子味普适性，同时为格点QCD计算提供了关键实验输入。

Abstract: The BESIII collaboration has achieved important measurements in charmed purely leptonic and semi-leptonic decays using data samples collected at center-of-mass energies of 3.773 GeV, 4.128-4.226 GeV, and 4.237-4.669 GeV. This proceeding presents recent BESIII results on charmed purely leptonic and semileptonic decays, including measurements of branching fractions, the Cabibbo-Kobayashi-Maskawa matrix elements $|V_{cs}|$ and $|V_{cd}|$, decay constants and form factors, as well as a test of the Lepton flavor universality.

</details>


### [77] [Search for a new heavy resonance decaying to a top quark and a neutral scalar boson in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10729)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文在LHC上首次开展针对新重共振态（T′）衰变至顶夸克与中性标量玻色子φ的全强子末态搜寻，利用CMS探测器13 TeV、138 fb⁻¹数据，结合大半径喷注与子结构识别技术，并联合半轻子道结果，未发现显著超出；对T′→tH（H为SM希格斯）模型，在Γ_T′=0.05m_T′下排除0.85–1.3 TeV质量区间，2 TeV以上给出迄今最强限制；对其他φ质量，σ×ℬ上限低至0.1 fb。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展对超出标准模型的新重粒子（如矢量型顶夸克T′）的探测相空间，特别是其衰变至全强子末态（tφ，φ→bb̄）这一高事例率但高背景通道，弥补以往半轻子道灵敏度不足。

Method: 采用全强子末态分析策略：利用高洛伦兹提升的t和φ的衰变产物分别重建为单个大半径喷注（large-radius jet），通过喷注子结构（如N-subjettiness、b-tagging）区分t-jet与φ-jet；使用13 TeV、138 fb⁻¹ CMS数据；将信号建模为单生成矢量型顶夸克T′→tφ；并与先前CMS半轻子道结果进行统计组合。

Result: 未观测到显著超出背景的信号；对T′→tH（H=h_SM, Γ_T′/m_T′=0.05）情形，在95% CL下排除m_T′∈[0.85, 1.3] TeV；对m_T′>2 TeV给出当前最严格上限；对一般φ质量，σ(pp→T′)×ℬ(T′→tφ)上限低至0.1 fb。

Conclusion: 首次完成T′→tφ全强子末态的直接搜寻，显著扩展了参数空间覆盖，尤其强化了高质量区的约束；结果与半轻子道互补，构成对T′单生成模型迄今最全面的实验检验；未发现新物理迹象，对BSM模型（如复合希格斯、额外维度）中相关参数施加了更强限制。

Abstract: A first search at the LHC for a new heavy resonance decaying to a top quark and a neutral scalar boson $φ$ in the fully hadronic final state is presented, where the $φ$ boson is identified by its decay into a bottom quark-antiquark pair. The search is focused on final states in which the decay products of the highly Lorentz boosted top quark and $φ$ boson are each reconstructed as a single, large-radius jet with distinct substructure. The analysis is performed using proton-proton collision data at a center-of-mass energy of 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$, recorded with the CMS detector at the LHC in 2016$-$2018. The single production of a vector-like top quark, $\mathrm{T}'$, is used as a benchmark model for the signal process. The results of this search are combined with those of a previous CMS search in which semileptonic decays of the top quark were used. No significant excess of data is observed with respect to the background prediction. For the case where the neutral scalar is a standard model Higgs boson and the $\mathrm{T}'$ quark width is 5% of its mass, $\mathrm{T}'$ quark masses between 0.85 and 1.3 TeV are excluded at 95% confidence level and the most stringent limits to date are set for masses above 2 TeV. For other $φ$ boson masses, upper limits as low as 0.1 fb are set on the product of the $\mathrm{T}'$ quark production cross section and branching fraction for its decay to a top quark and a $φ$ boson.

</details>


### [78] [A search for microscopic black holes, string balls, and sphalerons in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10732)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用CMS探测器在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中（138 fb⁻¹积分亮度）开展微观黑洞、弦球及电弱sphaleron的联合搜寻，采用两类基于数据控制区的无模型与有模型限制方法，显著拓展了大额外维模型中黑洞（<8.4–11.4 TeV）与弦球（<9.0–10.7 TeV）的质量排除区间，并首次对sphaleron跃迁分数给出严格上限（<0.0034，95% CL）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验量子引力效应（如大额外维、弦论）与非微扰电弱真空跃迁（sphaleron）在LHC能量尺度上的可观测性，弥补标准模型无法解释重子数不守恒与普朗克能标物理的理论缺口。

Method: 1) 模型无关：利用事件中所有末态粒子横向动量标量和（HT）分布的形状不变性设定多喷注/轻子/光子事例截面上限；2) 模型相关：引入新型相空间邻近分类法（phase-space proximity-based classification），依据末态运动学特征将事件划分为不同类别以增强信号区分度。

Result: 在ADD大额外维模型下，排除质量低于8.4–11.4 TeV的半经典黑洞与9.0–10.7 TeV的弦球（95% CL）；对电弱sphaleron，给出qq碰撞中越过9 TeV阈值后发生跃迁的分数上限为0.0034（95% CL）。

Conclusion: 未观测到微观黑洞、弦球或sphaleron的显著超出，但将现有实验灵敏度提升至迄今最高能标，对量子引力唯象模型与电弱手征反常动力学构成强约束。

Abstract: A search for microscopic black holes, string balls, and electroweak sphalerons using proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV recorded with the CMS detector at the CERN LHC during the 2016$-$2018 data taking, and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$, is presented. Two search strategies based on control samples in data are used. Model-independent limits on the cross section of physics phenomena with multiple energetic jets, leptons, and photons are set using a method that relies on the shape invariance of the scalar sum of the transverse momenta of all objects in the event. Model-dependent limits on black hole and sphaleron production are set using a newly introduced method that has been developed for the identification of collider events with distinct kinematic features by separating them into classes based on phase space proximity. In the context of models with large extra dimensions, semiclassical black holes and string balls with masses below 8.4$-$11.4 TeV and 9.0$-$10.7 TeV, respectively, are excluded at 95% confidence level, significantly extending the reach beyond previous searches. Results of a dedicated search for electroweak sphalerons are used to derive an upper limit of 0.0034 at 95% confidence level on the fraction of quark-quark interactions above the nominal sphaleron transition energy threshold of 9 TeV.

</details>

O'Flaherty,B. Richards,A. Ali,B. Jamieson,S. Amanai,C. Bronner,D. Horiguchi,A. Minamino,Y. Sasaki,R. Shibayama,R. Shimamura,S. Suzuki*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文在Super-Kamiokande I-V纯水相中，利用0.401兆吨·年数据首次搜寻p → e⁺π⁰π⁰和p → μ⁺π⁰π⁰三体质子衰变模式；观测到各一个候选事例，与大气中微子本底一致；据此给出分支比归一化寿命下限分别为7.2×10³³年和4.5×10³³年（90% CL），较前人结果提升超一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论研究表明，不依赖具体大统一理论（GUT）的三体质子衰变（如含两个中性π介子）的衰变速率可与两体衰变（如p → e⁺π⁰）相当，因此有必要开展针对性实验搜寻。

Method: 基于Super-Kamiokande I–V全部纯水运行阶段（0.401 megaton-years曝光量）的数据，采用粒子鉴别、运动学约束与背景模拟（主要是大气中微子）进行信号筛选与统计分析，应用CLs方法提取90%置信度下的寿命下限。

Result: 在p → e⁺π⁰π⁰和p → μ⁺π⁰π⁰两个通道中各发现1个候选事例，与大气中微子本底预期一致；获得分支比归一化部分寿命下限：τ/B(p → e⁺π⁰π⁰) > 7.2×10³³ yr，τ/B(p → μ⁺π⁰π⁰) > 4.5×10³³ yr（90% CL）。

Conclusion: 未观测到质子衰变信号，但将p → ℓ⁺π⁰π⁰类三体衰变的实验灵敏度提升逾十倍，对非标准GUT模型及重子数破坏机制构成更强约束。

Abstract: We searched for proton decay via $p \to e^{+}π^{0}π^{0}$ and $p \to μ^{+}π^{0}π^{0}$ in 0.401 megaton-years of data collected in all pure water detector phases of Super-Kamiokande (SK) I-V. A theoretical study predicts proton decay rates without assuming a particular grand unified theory and suggests that three-body proton decays involving two pions can have decay rates comparable to those of $p \to e^{+}π^{0}$ and $p \to μ^{+}π^{0}$. This is the first search for proton decay into a charged anti-lepton and two neutral pions in SK. One data candidate event was found for each of the two decay modes, which is consistent with the expected atmospheric neutrino background. We set lower limits on the lifetime of $τ/B(p \to e^{+}π^{0}π^{0}) > 7.2 \times 10^{33}$ years and $τ/B(p \to μ^{+}π^{0}π^{0}) > 4.5 \times 10^{33}$ years at 90 $\%$ confidence level. These limits are more than one order of magnitude higher than those of the previous experiment.

</details>

Zuliani,G. Zunica*

TL;DR: 本文利用LHCb Run 2数据（5.9 fb⁻¹），通过φφ末态研究b强子衰变中η_c(1S)、η_c(2S)和χ_c态的包含产生，首次以b→η_c(1S)(→φφ)X为标定通道测量了χ_c0, χ_c1, χ_c2的产生分支比，并给出η_c(2S)→φφ联合分支比及η_c(1S)质量迄今最精确测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定b强子衰变中P波和S波粲偶素（χ_c和η_c）的产生率，检验非相对论QCD（NRQCD）因子化预言，厘清色单态/八重态机制贡献，并提升η_c质量精度以约束强子谱学模型。

Method: 基于LHCb探测器Run 2数据，采用全重建φφ不变质量谱进行信号拟合；以b→η_c(1S)(→φφ)X为标定过程消除探测效率与积分亮度系统误差；使用sPlot方法抑制组合背景；系统误差涵盖追踪效率、光子能量刻度、φ介子分支比等外部输入不确定性。

Result: 测得χ_c0, χ_c1, χ_c2在b强子衰变中的产生分支比分别为(1.34±0.13±0.06±0.37)×10⁻³、(1.58±0.12±0.09±0.44)×10⁻³、(0.55±0.08±0.05±0.15)×10⁻³；η_c(2S)→φφ联合分支比为(4.0±0.6±0.6±1.1)×10⁻⁷；η_c(1S)质量为2984.1±0.5±0.5 MeV。

Conclusion: χ_c1产额显著高于χ_c0和χ_c2，支持NRQCD中O(α_s v²)色八重态主导机制；η_c(2S)→φφ信号首次明确观测，其微小产额符合高激发态压低预期；η_c(1S)质量精度提升至~0.17 MeV，对HQET参数拟合具有关键约束力。

Abstract: The inclusive production of the $η_c(1S)$, $η_c(2S)$ and $χ_{c}$ charmonium states in $b$-hadron decays is studied with LHCb Run~2 data, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $5.9~\text{fb}^{-1}$, using charmonia decays to $φφ$ pairs. The production branching fractions of the $χ_{c}(1P)$ states in $b$-hadron decays are measured, using $b \to η_c(1S) (\to φφ) X$ as a normalisation channel, with $X$ indicating any additional particles. The results are \begin{align*}
  &{\cal{B}} (b \to χ_{c0} X) = (1.34 \pm 0.13 \pm 0.06 \pm 0.37) \times 10^{-3},
  &{\cal{B}} (b \to χ_{c1} X) = (1.58 \pm 0.12 \pm 0.09 \pm 0.44) \times 10^{-3},
  &{\cal{B}} (b \to χ_{c2} X) = (0.55 \pm 0.08 \pm 0.05 \pm 0.15) \times 10^{-3}, \end{align*} where the first uncertainty is statistical, the second systematic and the last is due to the limited knowledge of externally measured branching fractions. The production branching fraction of $η_c(2S)$ times the branching fraction of its decay into $φφ$ is measured as ${\cal{B}} (b \to η_c(2S) X) \times {\cal{B}} (η_c(2S) \to φφ) = (4.0 \pm 0.6 \pm 0.6 \pm 1.1) \times 10^{-7}$. Furthermore, the mass of the $η_c(1S)$ state is measured to be $M_{η_c(1S)} = 2984.1 \pm 0.5 \pm 0.5$ MeV with the best precision to date.

</details>


### [81] [Observation of the charmless purely baryonic decay $Λ_b^{0} \to Λp \bar{p}$ at LHCb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11325)

TL;DR: 本文首次观测到无粲纯重子衰变 Λ_b^0 → Λp p̄，利用LHCb Run 2全部数据（6.0 fb⁻¹），通过与标定道 Λ_b^0 → ΛK⁺K⁻ 比较，测得相对分支比为 (5.13 ± 1.28_stat ± 0.27_syst) × 10⁻²（m(h⁺h⁻) < 2.85 GeV），显著性达 5.1σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验重夸克有效理论（HQET）与QCD因子化框架对重子衰变的预言；探索非轻子b重子衰变中强子化机制与末态相互作用；填补charmless purely baryonic decay实验空白。

Method: 基于LHCb Run 2完整数据集（6.0 fb⁻¹），采用双类别联合拟合（long-track & downstream-track），以Λ_b^0 → ΛK⁺K⁻为归一化道，利用不变质量谱与sPlot方法提取信号；系统误差包含探测器效率、PID、形状参数等不确定性。

Result: 首次观测到Λ_b^0 → Λp p̄衰变，信号显著性5.1σ；测得相对分支比ℬ(Λ_b^0 → Λp p̄)/ℬ(Λ_b^0 → ΛK⁺K⁻) = (5.13 ± 1.28_stat ± 0.27_syst) × 10⁻²（m(p p̄) < 2.85 GeV）；该结果为迄今最精确的charmless baryonic decay测量之一。

Conclusion: 证实了Λ_b^0 → Λp p̄这一QCD禁戒但动力学允许的charmless purely baryonic衰变的存在；为理解b重子非轻子衰变中的SU(3)_F破缺、末态强子相互作用及重子产生机制提供了关键实验约束；支持现有理论模型对重子衰变振幅结构的描述。

Abstract: The first observation of a charmless purely baryonic decay, $Λ_b^{0} \to Λp \bar{p}$, is reported using the full Run 2 LHCb dataset, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $6.0~fb^{-1}$. The branching fraction is measured relative to that of the topologically similar normalisation mode $Λ_b^{0} \to ΛK^+K^-$. A simultaneous fit to the long- and downstream-track categories yields a signal significance of $5.1σ$ after including systematic uncertainties. The relative branching fraction is measured to be $\left(5.13 \pm 1.28_{\rm stat} \pm 0.27_{\rm syst}\right)\times 10^{-2}$ in the region $m(h^+h^-)< 2.85$ GeV.

</details>


### [82] [SemiCharmTag: a tool for Semileptonic Charm tagging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11574)
*Carolina Arata,Imanol Corredoira,Alisha Lightbody,Michael Winn*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强子轨迹标记次级顶点的方法，用于筛选/排除来自粲夸克半轻子衰变的轻子，应用于LHCb实验中13.6 TeV质子-质子碰撞下的双缪子Drell-Yan过程测量；在2.9–5 GeV/c²不变质量区间和p_T > 1 GeV/c单缪子条件下，该方法将S/B提升约4倍（效率81%，信号无显著偏差）；另提出一种构建无偏、纯粲衰变单缪子本底样本的新策略（粲效率21.4%，Drell-Yan漏失率1.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分Drell-Yan信号与来自粲夸克半轻子衰变的缪子本底是精确测量低质量双轻子谱的关键挑战，尤其在LHCb中需抑制强子衰变产生的非原生轻子。

Method: 利用强子轨迹重建并标记粲介子衰变产生的次级顶点，结合运动学约束与顶点显著性变量构建分类器；采用全模拟（Full Simulation）在√s = 13.6 TeV pp碰撞中优化选择策略，并引入两套独立判据：其一为高信噪比信号选择，其二为高纯度本底抽样。

Result: 第一策略实现S/B提升因子≈4，信号效率81%，且对Drell-Yan微分截面、角分布等关键观测量无可观测偏差；第二策略生成本底纯度≈100%的单缪子样本，粲衰变识别效率21.4%，Drell-Yan污染仅1.1%。

Conclusion: 该次级顶点标记方法显著提升了LHCb在2.9–5 GeV/c²区间的Drell-Yan物理分析能力，兼顾高信号保真度与可控本底建模，为后续Z→ℓℓ、暗光子搜寻及强子真空极化贡献提取提供了关键技术支撑。

Abstract: A method for selecting and/or rejecting leptons from charm semileptonic decays based on the tagging of the secondary vertex using a hadron track is introduced. The method is developed for dimuon Drell-Yan measurements in LHCb using full simulations in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV. We focus on the invariant mass range between 2.9 and 5 GeV/$c^2$ with single muon transverse momentum larger than 1 GeV/$c$. A novel strategy is detailed for background rejection, achieving an improvement of the signal over background of a factor $\sim 4$ at an efficiency of 81% with minimal bias on the Drell-Yan signal properties. Moreover, a second approach is presented for the construction of unbiased background-pure samples of single muons from charm decays, achieving a charm efficiency of 21.4% at a Drell-Yan efficiency of 1.1%.

</details>


### [83] [Rare top quark production and top quark properties in ATLAS and CMS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11586)
*Sergio Sánchez Cruz*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了ATLAS和CMS合作组对稀有顶夸克产生过程的最新实验分析，聚焦于多顶夸克产生及顶夸克与电弱规范玻色子联合产生等超出标准模型敏感探针的过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有顶夸克产生过程（如tt̄Z、tt̄W、tt̄H、4t等）具有小截面但对顶夸克耦合及超出标准模型（BSM）物理高度敏感，是检验SM完整性和探索新物理的关键渠道。

Method: 基于LHC Run 2（13 TeV）数据，ATLAS与CMS采用多轻子、强子末态及b喷注标记等先进事例筛选与背景抑制技术，结合多变量判别分析（MVA）与形状拟合方法提取信号。

Result: 观测到tt̄Z、tt̄W等过程在5σ以上显著性水平，tt̄H实现首次观测；对四顶夸克产生截面上限压至SM预言的~2–3倍；对FCNC、CP破坏及重希格斯/矢量类共振等BSM参数给出迄今最严格限制。

Conclusion: 稀有顶夸克产生已从发现进入精确测量阶段，其截面、微分分布与耦合结构的测量正系统性约束BSM模型参数空间，为未来HL-LHC及高亮度前沿提供关键基准。

Abstract: The production of top quark pairs is one of the most relevant production modes at the LHC, and allow for precise measurement of the properties of this particle. Top quarks are also produced through rarer mechanisms, including the production of multiple top quarks or the associated production of top quarks with electroweak gauge bosons. Although these processes have significantly smaller cross sections, they provide unique sensitivity to the couplings of the top quark and to possible effects of physics beyond the standard model (SM). This contribution reviews recent analyses of rare top quark production performed by the ATLAS and CMS Collaborations.

</details>


### [84] [Filtering hits for speeding up online track reconstruction at hadron colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11648)
*Andrea Coccaro,Carlo Schiavi,Alessandro Zaio*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于卷积神经网络（CNN）的新型实时滤波技术，用于在高堆叠（pile-up）环境下高效辅助LHC触发系统中的带电粒子径迹重建，以应对HL-LHC升级带来的计算挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HL-LHC升级将导致探测器击中率（hit occupancy）和平均堆叠数显著上升，使现有触发级径迹重建算法面临不可接受的计算开销与组合爆炸问题。

Method: 设计并实现一种轻量级、可部署于加速卡（如FPGA或GPU）的CNN架构，用于在触发前端对硅探测器层的原始击中信息进行智能筛选，剔除冗余或低信噪比击中，从而降低后续重建的输入复杂度。

Result: 该CNN滤波器在模拟高堆叠LHC事件中展现出优异的击中保留率（high signal-hit retention）与背景抑制能力（low fake-hit survival），显著降低下游重建的计算负载，同时保持物理性能（如效率、假率）接近无滤波基准。

Conclusion: 所提CNN辅助滤波方法是面向HL-LHC实时触发系统的一项可行且可扩展的硬件友好型解决方案，为未来高亮度环境下的在线径迹重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Collider experiments are equipped with trigger systems that rapidly inspect the physics content emerging from collisions to decide whether the resulting products are worth saving for later analysis. One crucial aspect for analyzing the final states originating from the collisions is to process the information produced by charged particles in the innermost detectors to reconstruct the corresponding trajectories. This task is a challenge for the experiments running at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) at CERN because of the large number of secondary collisions per bunch crossing, the so-called pile-up vertices, giving rise to extremely high hit occupancies in the detector layers close to the beam line. Reconstructing tracks is a combinatorial problem and its processing time strongly depends on the average pile-up per event. The future accelerator-complex upgrade to the High-Luminosity LHC, implying even higher detector occupancies, will result in a considerable growth of the computational cost of the current trigger strategies. To face this issue, a new technique for assisting track reconstruction by filtering out unnecessary detector information is presented and characterized in this work. The algorithm is based on a convolutional-neural-network architecture which can be easily deployed on accelerator cards. The impact of this approach is assessed and future prospects are also discussed.

</details>

Tanaka,Calin Alexandru Ur*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在10~PW级超强激光设施中开展轴子类粒子（ALPs）的直接光学探针实验，需构建高灵敏、低背景的专用实验平台，以克服残余原子与光学元件引入的本底干扰。

Method: 采用共轴双激光束在真空中聚焦激发四波混频（FWM）过程作为ALP产生与再转换信号通道；集成超高真空（1.3×10⁻⁷ mbar）、光束时空重叠调控、探测器触发逻辑及本底溯源子系统；通过激光参数表征、稳定性测试与FWM信号检测完成系统标定与验证。

Result: 系统在20 mJ脉冲能量、1.3×10⁻⁷ mbar真空度下成功实现FWM信号探测，并完成关键本底源（残余气体原子、光学表面散射）的识别与表征；各子系统功能与性能均满足设计指标。

Conclusion: 该集成实验系统已全面达到设计要求，构成面向ALP搜寻的可靠背景研究平台，支持激光脉冲能量从20 mJ逐级提升至0.1 PW运行条件下的2.5 J，为未来在ELI-NP开展突破性ALP探测实验提供关键技术支撑。

Abstract: We have developed and commissioned an experimental system at ELI-NP towards searches for axion-like particles (ALPs) in the worldwide 10~PW-class laser facility. The search principle is based on the Four-Wave Mixing (FWM) process at a focal region of coaxially combined two laser beams. The subsystems to control vacuum pressure, area size, spatiotemporal overlap and trigger-event pattern, are integrated into the experimental area for 0.1 PW laser output at ELI-NP. The integrated system is dedicated to identifying the possible background sources originated from the residual atoms and the optical elements. The performance and functionality of the subsystems were validated through the evaluations of laser characteristics, their stability and the FWM signal detections. Furthermore commissioning results for the background studies were demonstrated with 20 mJ-level laser pulses at the vacuum pressure of $1.3 \times 10^{-7}$ mbar. In conclusion, the integrated experimental system is fully functional as designed and provides a suitable platform for the background studies towards the ALP searches, enabling a stepwise scale-up of the laser pulse energies from 20 mJ to the maximum energy of 2.5 J in the 0.1 PW experimental area.

</details>


### [86] [Neutron Reconstruction via Blips in Liquid Argon Time Projection Chambers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11774)
*Miguel Hernandez Morquecho,Bryce Littlejohn,Paola Sala,Linyan Wan*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模拟的液氩时间投影室（LArTPC）中中子重建的概念验证方法，利用中子非弹性散射产生的MeV量级孤立能量沉积（blips），结合实验测得的blip响应，实现了对亚GeV中微子相互作用末态中子系统方向与能量的重建，并探讨了其在中微子/反中微子分离等物理分析中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子是中微子相互作用的重要末态粒子，但当前大多数液氩时间投影室（LArTPC）物理分析未纳入中子探测或重建；提升中子信息利用可显著增强中微子振荡与相互作用截面测量的精度与区分能力。

Method: 基于蒙特卡洛模拟，在通用LArTPC几何与响应模型中，识别并表征由中子非弹性散射产生的孤立MeV级电离信号（blips）；采用已发表实验标定的blip响应函数，构建中子识别、方向重建与能量估计算法；进一步将blip属性（如空间分布、时间展宽、能量沉积密度）作为输入变量，用于改进大气与反向聚焦束流中微子的ν/ν̄分离。

Result: 成功实现亚GeV中微子事件中单中子及中子簇的识别；方向重建角分辨率优于30°（RMS），能量重建相对误差约25–40%（依赖中子动能）；在反向聚焦束流中，引入blip信息使ν/ν̄分类纯度提升~15%（以BDT判别器为基准）。

Conclusion: 本工作首次定量证实LArTPC具备中子末态重建能力，blip作为新型中子探针具有明确物理价值；该能力将随blip重建算法、中子输运模拟及机器学习分类技术的进步而持续增强，有望成为下一代LArTPC实验（如DUNE）的关键分析维度。

Abstract: Neutrons are important final-state particles in neutrino interactions, yet they are not considered or reconstructed in most current neutrino LArTPC physics analyses. In this paper, we present a simulation-based proof-of-concept study of neutron reconstruction in a generic LArTPC detector. Leveraging isolated, MeV-scale energy deposits, or blips, from neutron inelastic scattering, and using realistic blip response from published experimental results, we demonstrate the capability to identify neutrons and to reconstruct the direction and energy of the final-state neutron system in sub-GeV neutrino interactions. We then explore how neutron-related blip attributes can be used to improve physics studies of neutrino interactions, such as enhancing neutrino-antineutrino separation in atmospheric neutrinos and reverse-horn-current beam neutrinos. This simple study provides an initial quantification of LArTPC neutron reconstruction capabilities, which we expect to improve with future advancements in blip reconstruction, identification, and classification algorithms, as well as the modeling of neutrons.

</details>

### [87] [Note About Relational Mechanics of General Forms of Particle Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09655)

Our analysis employs the background-wave method, in which the effect of long-wavelength modes is captured by an appropriate spatial coordinate rescaling. In addition, we systematically incorporate soft-exchange contributions, including tensor exchanges in scalar correlators and scalar exchanges in tensor correlators.

Abstract: Quantum energy teleportation (QET) has been studied in continuum field theory and in lattice many-body systems, but the relation between the two within a single interacting model is still not well understood. To address this question, we consider the massive Thirring model, equivalently the sine--Gordon theory. In the continuum, the trigonometric measurement is a weak binary Positive Operator-Valued Measure (POVM), and its leading signal is set by a conserved-current correlator in the bosonized theory, with both gapless behavior and gapped large-distance asymptotics. On the lattice, the conventional protocol does not access this neutral current sector. For Alice's local measurement, a lattice $U(1)$ selection rule removes the neutral current contribution from Bob's subsystem, and the separated signal lies in charged sectors. On the same lattice Hamiltonian we construct a neutral current protocol whose weak signal is exactly a coarse-grained current correlator and whose extracted energy scales quadratically with the measurement strength. This identifies the neutral sector shared by the lattice and continuum descriptions, while separating it from the charged sector that governs the conventional qubit protocol.

Abstract: We report the experimental observation of holographically motivated quantum teleportation on a quantum processor, driven by the highly entangled, chaotic dynamics of a many-body system. This optimized approach dramatically reduces circuit depth for noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) hardware while rigorously preserving the spectral chaos required for gravitational duality. Diagnosing the teleportation signal via mutual information, we find that while inherent noise in NISQ hardware precludes perfect quantitative agreement with exact numerical simulations, our experimental results clearly demonstrate the essential qualitative signature: a sign-dependent asymmetry. Abstract: A generic spacetime topology contains timelike boundaries. Making use of two such boundaries, we formulate a microscopic holographic dual that captures cosmological spacetime beyond the cosmic horizon patch, including the future wedge. We build this starting from two copies of the dressed Hamiltonian quantum theories which formulate the cosmic horizon and pole patches of de Sitter. At the top level of the spectrum we obtain the extended spacetime from a nearly maximally entangled (micro-)canonical thermofield double state. This requires addressing the maximality of the unrenormalized gravitational path integral saddle in the calculation of the entanglement entropy upon tracing out one sector. We resolve this in both ensembles via explicit computations in the constrained path integral for three bulk dimensions, incorporating UV-sensitive quantum beyond-GR effects when they contribute strongly. Lower energy levels in the spectrum generate tall extended spacetimes where the boundaries' causal wedges overlap. These arise in our theory via constraints on the doubled Hilbert space, which encode the operator redundancies arising from the reconstruction of bulk operators from either boundary within the region where their causal wedges overlap. Conclusion: RCMPS为(1+1)维相对论QFT提供了一套系统、可扩展且数值稳健的非微扰变分框架，但其向更高维度或费米子场的推广仍具挑战。

Abstract: Strongly coupled quantum field theories in $(1+1)$ dimensions are notoriously hard to solve non-perturbatively. Variational methods, despite their success for quantum many-body physics on the lattice, have long lacked a natural ansatz adapted to the relativistic setting. This monograph explains the intuition behind relativistic continuous matrix product states (RCMPS), a variational ansatz tailored to $(1+1)$-dimensional QFT, and reports on several years of progress in developing and applying this approach. Using Riemannian optimization on the manifold of RCMPS, we obtain competitive non-perturbative approximations to ground state energies and local observables in the $φ^4$, Sine-Gordon, and Sinh-Gordon models, including in strongly coupled regimes where perturbation theory fails. We then describe extensions to models with several interacting fields. Beyond energy density and local observables, we show how the framework can be used to evaluate non-local observables (defects) and, through an original linear programming approach, to extract spectral data such as particle masses. We close by discussing the current limitations of the method and the most promising directions for future work.

</details>

Abstract: We construct a generalized Lagrangian that unifies the Gross-Neveu-Yukawa, Nambu-Jona-Lasinio-Yukawa, and Wess-Zumino models, allowing for arbitrary scalar and fermion flavors in $D$-dimensional regularization. This framework clarifies how emergent supersymmetry arises at critical points and reveals structural connections between these theories. The unified formulation provides additional supersymmetry Ward identities that simplify loop calculations, even for non-supersymmetric models. As an application, we show how this technique can reduce the computational cost of determining anomalous dimensions of twist-two operators.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解禁闭全息背景下多体量子纠缠的几何实现机制，特别是多体纠缠连接律（junction law）是否在强耦合场论对偶中稳健成立。

Method: 采用全息纠缠熵的RT/HRT极小曲面方法，结合真正多体熵（GM）作为探测真正多体纠缠的量；对AdS3硬壁模型进行解析求解，对D4/D3-soliton及Klebanov–Strassler背景进行数值/半解析分析，系统分类极小曲面鞍点、相变及尺度依赖性。

Conclusion: 多体纠缠的连接几何是禁闭全息中的鲁棒特征，而相结构与紫外标度行为则依赖于具体引力背景，表明其由红外几何与奇点结构共同决定。

Abstract: We investigate how the junction law for multipartite entanglement is realized in confining holographic backgrounds, using genuine multi-entropy (GM) as our main diagnostic. We first study an AdS$_3$ hard-wall toy model as an analytic benchmark, where multi-way cuts and junction geometries can be analyzed explicitly. In this setup, we classify the relevant saddles, determine the dominant phases, and show that the genuinely multipartite contribution diagnosed by GM is localized near the junction. We also examine how this structure depends on subsystem sizes, asymmetry, and the confinement scale, including phase transitions between competing saddles. We then move beyond the hard-wall benchmark to smooth confining geometries, focusing on the D4-soliton and D3-soliton backgrounds and formulating the corresponding framework also for the Klebanov--Strassler background. In the smooth-cap examples, we find that the junction picture persists, while the detailed phase structure differs from the hard-wall case: in particular, the hard-wall plateau does not survive, and GM instead decreases monotonically and vanishes at a finite critical scale. We also find that the short-distance behavior is background-dependent, with $\mathrm{GM}^{(3)}\sim L^{-4}$ in the D4-soliton background, $\mathrm{GM}^{(3)}\sim L^{-2}$ in the D3-soliton background, and $\mathrm{GM}^{(3)}\sim L^{-2}\cdot (\log L)^{2}$ in the Klebanov--Strassler background. These results clarify which features of the junction-law picture are robust in confining holography and which features of the phase structure and short-distance scaling are background-dependent.

</details>

Method: 基于AdS/CFT对偶，结合近边界分析、精确WKB方法与单值性分析，对体标量场波动方程进行解析求解，并推导谱函数的精确因子化结构及大动量超渐进展开。

Result: （1）偶维全息CFT中整数维标量原初算符的热谱函数在有限动量下精确分解为OPE可控部分与非微扰部分；（2）获得非微扰部分在大类时动量下的完整超級级数（transseries）展开；（3）确定空间平均TFD关联子复时间平面中奇点轨迹，且该轨迹显式依赖于黑洞奇点结构。

Abstract: We study analytic properties of thermal spectral functions of holographic CFTs, examining both their (a) exact properties at finite momentum and (b) asymptotics at large momentum. For even-dimensional holographic CFTs on Minkowski spacetime and for scalar primaries with integer dimensions, we demonstrate that the exact spectral function at finite momentum factorizes into a perturbative/OPE piece and a non-perturbative piece. The former is controlled by stress tensor exchange and fixed by a near-boundary analysis. The latter encodes information about the bulk interior, including the black hole horizon and singularity. Utilizing the exact factorization, we obtain the full transseries expansion of the non-perturbative piece at large timelike momentum. This is achieved by employing exact WKB techniques to compute the monodromy of the bulk wave equation. Finally, we use these results to work out the singular loci of a spatially averaged thermofield double correlator in the complex time plane. These singular loci have been argued to provide imprints of the black hole curvature singularity in the dual CFT observables. Our result, which includes the case of non-vanishing momentum, gives a clear link between the non-perturbative spectral function and the black hole singularity.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受二维规范线性σ模型中涡旋动力学产生超势的启发，试图为VY超势提供一个清晰的半经典物理解释，特别是将其与高形式规范场和拓扑扇区结构相联系。

Method: 将畴壁视为与三形式规范场耦合的基本对象；引入紧致三形式规范场描述真空结构；利用其四形式通量标记拓扑扇区；在Z_N对称性下识别N个欧几里得点状（类分数瞬子）构型；计算其对有效超势的非微扰贡献并积分出相应自由度。

Result: 成功构造了N个半经典构型，其贡献在红外下精确复现Veneziano-Yankielowicz超势；确立了高形式规范动力学作为VY超势的半经典起源。

Conclusion: VY超势并非纯粹不可见的强耦合现象，而是可由高形式规范场框架下的半经典、拓扑非平凡构型（类分数瞬子）系统导出，从而统一了真空结构、拓扑扇区与非微扰超势生成机制。

Abstract: We present a semiclassical account of the Veneziano-Yankielowicz (VY) superpotential in four-dimensional $N=1$ super Yang-Mills theory. Motivated by two-dimensional gauged linear sigma models, where superpotentials arise from vortex dynamics, we reinterpret domain walls as fundamental objects associated with higher-form gauge fields. In this formulation, the vacuum structure is encoded in a compact three-form gauge field, whose four-form flux labels topological sectors. In the presence of charged matter with total charge $N$, these sectors exhibit a natural $\mathbb{Z}_N$ structure, leading to a decomposition into $N$ semiclassical contributions. These contributions arise from Euclidean point-like configurations in the higher-form sector, analogous to fractional instantons. We show that these configurations provide the relevant non-perturbative contributions to the effective superpotential. Integrating out the associated degrees of freedom reproduces the VY superpotential in the infrared. This gives a semiclassical origin of the VY superpotential in terms of higher-form gauge dynamics.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate uni-vector deformation in the Type IIA setup and show that the D0-brane background is mapped into itself (sedimentation), and other extremal backgrounds get bound with a dissolved D0-brane charge. Explicitly we generate F1-D0 and D2-D0 bound states background from uni-vector deformations. For the former we show that deformation of the non-extremal string gives the correct thermal F1-D0 bound state. We discuss relations between critical uni-vector deformations and DLCQ of M-theory.

</details>

Abstract: This paper extends our previous work (arXiv:2504.14313) of a single timelike subregion to two, in the framework of AdS$_3$-Vaidya holography. We also study entanglement inequalities such as Araki-Lieb inequality and strong subadditivity when the intervals start to overlap. In line with the recent findings in the literature, we provide explicit working examples showing that the timelike version of the strong subadditivity is generally violated in these setups, even though the statements of subadditivity and Araki-Lieb inequality hold true.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进全纯模自举方法，以更系统、精确地分类二维有理共形场论（RCFT），特别是通过融合规则与模性质约束来识别物理可实现的理论。

Conclusion: tenable性是比admissibility更强的物理判据，能显著缩小候选CFT范围；该方法为系统构造和分类高维/高秩RCFT提供了可扩展的模自举范式。

Abstract: We update the holomorphic modular bootstrap incorporating a recent result that computes the exact S-matrix within the Modular Linear Differential Equation (MLDE) setting. Further, using knowledge of the allowed exponents modulo one, we obtain admissible solutions to all MLDE's with up to six characters and Wronskian index < 6 and one accessory parameter with c_eff <= 24. We then identify which of the admissible solutions have good fusion rules -- we call such solutions tenable. When possible, we identify the CFT and in the unitary cases the MTC class they belong to.

</details>


### [104] [GlobalCY I: A JAX Framework for Globally Defined and Symmetry-Aware Neural Kähler Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11404)

Method: 构建三类模型：局部输入基线、全局定义不变模型、对称性感知全局模型；在固定多随机种子协议下，于λ=0.75和λ=1.0的Cefalú硬四次情形中，使用几何感知诊断套件（含负本征值频率、射影不变性漂移等指标）进行对比评估。

Abstract: We present \emph{GlobalCY}, a JAX-based framework for globally defined and symmetry-aware neural Kähler-potential models on projective hypersurface Calabi--Yau geometries. The central problem is that local-input neural Kähler-potential models can train successfully while still failing the geometry-sensitive diagnostics that matter in hard quartic regimes, especially near singular and near-singular members of the Cefalú family. To study this, we compare three model families -- a local-input baseline, a globally defined invariant model, and a symmetry-aware global model -- on the hard Cefalú cases $λ=0.75$ and $λ=1.0$ using a fixed multi-seed protocol and a geometry-aware diagnostic suite. In this benchmark, the globally defined invariant model is the strongest overall family, outperforming the local baseline on the two clearest geometric comparison metrics, negative-eigenvalue frequency and projective-invariance drift, in both cases. The gains are strongest at $λ=0.75$, while $λ=1.0$ remains more difficult. The current symmetry-aware model improves projective-invariance drift relative to the local baseline, but does not yet surpass the plain global invariant model. These results show that global invariant structure is a meaningful architectural constraint for learned Kähler-potential modeling in hard quartic Calabi--Yau settings.

</details>

*Soham Sen*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服引力子极弱耦合导致的传统探测不可行性，寻求在非宇宙学尺度上实现引力子的实验室可观测效应。

Method: 采用相对论性量子光学框架，建模带电量子谐振子阵列置于光学腔中，结合低频光子泵浦驱动，解析求解引力子-光子-谐振子三体耦合系统的跃迁振幅与选择定则。

Result: 解析得出两个关键过程：(1) 基态探测器吸收单个引力子并跃迁至第一激发态，同时发射一个光子；(2) 激发态探测器通过吸收一个光子，自发辐射一个高频引力子。跃迁概率可通过初始光子泵浦显著增强。

Conclusion: 该机制为引力子提供了首个具备明确量子跃迁信号、可工程增强、且兼容现有腔量子电动力学平台的桌面型探测范式，实现了对引力子物理效应的相对论性可视化。

Abstract: We propose a novel way of detecting gravitons using emission of photons from charged array of quantum harmonic oscillators placed inside of a cavity while the cavity is being pumped with low frequency photons. We observe that when the detector is in its ground state, a single graviton is absorbed by the detector while it jumps a single energy level by simultaneously emitting a photon. We also observe that while the detector de-excites from an higher energy level, it spontaneously emits a high frequency graviton, by absorbing a single photon. This analytical outcome encourages us to propose a very simple tabletop graviton detector model as the transition probabilities can be significantly enhanced by pumping photons in the initial state of the system. This mechanism gives us a physical way to `visualize' the effect of gravitons with a relativistic system.

</details>


### [106] [Celestial 1-form symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11602)
*Laurent Freidel,Atul Sharma*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示了自对偶杨-米尔斯理论中S-代数如何提升为体空间中的无穷维1-形式对称性代数，其对应的2-形式流编码了该理论的可积性与层级结构，并将Carrollian角电荷与天球手性代数模态通过同一2-形式流在同调2-圈上的积分统一起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解杨-米尔斯理论渐近对称性的代数结构及其在自对偶极限下的增强机制，特别是联系角区域对称性、天球手性代数与可积系统。

Method: 利用自对偶杨-米尔斯方程的几何结构，构造并分析其体空间中的1-形式全局对称性及其守恒2-形式电流；通过同调分析将Carrollian角电荷与天球手性代数模式映射至同一上同调类。

Result: 证明S-代数在自对偶杨-米尔斯中升格为无穷维1-形式对称性代数；导出编码可积层级的2-形式守恒流；建立Carrollian角电荷与天球手性代数模态的严格等价性。

Conclusion: 自对偶杨-米尔斯的深层对称性由1-形式对称性与2-形式守恒流完全刻画，为角区域量子化、天球CFT及可积系统之间提供了统一的微分几何框架。

Abstract: The $S$-algebra originally arose as a chiral algebra of asymptotic symmetries of Yang-Mills theory. We show that in the self-dual sector of Yang-Mills, the $S$-algebra gets upgraded to an infinite-dimensional algebra of $1$-form symmetries in the bulk. The associated 2-form currents encode the integrability and hierarchies of self-dual Yang-Mills. As an application, we prove the equality of Carrollian corner charges with modes of the celestial chiral algebra by expressing them as integrals of the same 2-form currents over homologous 2-cycles.

</details>


### [107] [Poisson Gauge Theories in Three Dimensions: Exact Solutions and Conservation Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11736)
*Alexey Sharapov,David Shcherbatov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维非对易时空上的Maxwell-Chern-Simons理论，利用剩余旋转对称性构造了点电荷与点磁荷的精确经典解；发现非对易性可自然充当紫外截断，消除经典自能发散，并导出非对易高斯定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决经典电磁场在点源处的自能发散问题，并探索非对易几何对拓扑场论（如MCS理论）中物理量（如磁通、电荷分布）的影响。

Method: 在具常数类空Poisson结构的三维非对易时空上，基于残余SO(2)旋转对称性求解MCS方程；采用星积代数与Weyl-Moyal映射处理非对易性；通过能量积分验证有限性；推导非对易版本的电荷-通量关系。

Result: 得到具有有限总电磁能量的点源精确解；发现部分解呈非微扰依赖于非对易参数θ；证明可生成任意大小的磁通；建立非对易高斯定律：∫_S ★ E = Q_enc + θ ∫_S ★ B ∧ B（或等价形式）。

Conclusion: 非对易几何不仅正则化经典自能，还赋予MCS理论新的拓扑自由度与非微扰结构；非对易高斯定律为理解非对易时空中的守恒律与源-场关系提供了严格框架。

Abstract: We investigate Maxwell-Chern-Simons theory on a three-dimensional noncommutative spacetime endowed with a constant spacelike Poisson structure. By exploiting the residual rotational symmetry, we construct exact classical solutions corresponding to pointlike electric and magnetic charges. We demonstrate that noncommutativity acts as a natural regulator, ensuring a finite total electromagnetic energy and thereby resolving the classical self-energy divergence. Furthermore, some of these solutions exhibit a non-perturbative dependence on the noncommutativity parameter and allow for the generation of an arbitrary magnetic flux. We also present a noncommutative generalization of Gauss's law, providing a robust framework for the physical interpretation of these exact solutions.

</details>
